13739               	;
13740               	; 	' THE COLOUR TABLE, 472 bytes of the same value ($14 = black ink on dark blue).
13741               	; 	'
13742               	; 	' Loaded with DEFINE COLOR 32,59,font_col, which is ONE synchronous call that
13743               	; 	' writes all three screen thirds (define_color always does the LDIRVM3 triple
13744               	; 	' copy). It replaced a VPOKE loop that wrote this one value 1,416 times paced by
13745               	; 	' 24 WAITs -- the largest scripted delay in the boot, and the reason the title
13746               	; 	' screen used to fill in visibly rather than appearing at once.
13747               	; 	'
13748               	; 	' EIGHT BYTES PER CHARACTER, not one. Supply fewer and DEFINE COLOR reads
13749               	; 	' whatever follows in ROM as colour data, with no error at build or run time.
13750               	;
13751               	; font_col:
13752               cvb_FONT_COL
13753               	; 	DATA BYTE $14,$14,$14,$14,$14,$14,$14,$14	' space

13871               	; 	INCLUDE "art.bas"
13872               	; 	' Keystone Kapers art -- GENERATED by assets/genart.py. Do not edit; regenerate.
13873               	; 	'
13874               	; 	' EVERY SPRITE IS 16x16: sprite size and magnification are GLOBAL on the
13875               	; 	' TMS9918, so small objects are drawn small INSIDE a 16x16 box rather than
13876               	; 	' being small sprites.
13877               	; 	'
13878               	; 	' EVERY 8-PX OBJECT SITS IN ROWS 8-15 OF ITS BOX, ducked Kelly included, so
13879               	; 	' one rule places them all: bottom edge = y + 16, and a thing resting on the
13880               	; 	' floor is at y = FLOORY - 16.
13881               	; 	'
13882               	; 	' Sprite bytes are QUADRANT-ORDERED: 16 rows of the left half, then 16 of
13883               	; 	' the right. Every block is an EVEN number of bytes -- an odd DATA BYTE run
13884               	; 	' silently misaligns every word table after it (CLAUDE.md 3A).
13885               	;
13886               	; spr_kelly:	' Kelly: RIGHT hat/face/tunic/tunic-arm-up/duck-hat/duck-face, then the same LEFT (+24), then the FOUR shared run frames. Patterns 0..60
13887               cvb_SPR_KELLY
13888               	; 	DATA BYTE $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00
13889 63DA 0000     	byte >00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00

     63DE 0000     
     63E0 0000

14883               	;
14884               	; 	' -------------------------------------------------- scanner canvas
14885               	; 	' Chars 208-255: 16 cols x 3 rows over screen rows 21-23. Blank to start;
14886               	; 	' dots are plotted by VPOKEing the PATTERN table (rows 21-23 sit in the
14887               	; 	' third screen third, so every scanner pattern is at base 4096).
14888               	;
14889               	; 	' --------------------------------------------------- font colours
14890               	; 	' Chars 32-90, EIGHT bytes each. Without this the font keeps whatever
14891               	; 	' CVBasic left in the colour table, which on a green store made the HUD
14892               	; 	' unreadable.
14893               	;
14894               	; scan_col3:	' one 8-byte block per canvas row: 4 px of GREY margin, then a 3 px band of furniture GREY and a yellow floor line, per 4 px level, on dark green. The Kop and the crook are coloured PER CHARACTER at run time -- see scan_set
14895               cvb_SCAN_COL3
14896               	; 	DATA BYTE $EE,$EE,$EE,$EE,$EC,$EC,$EC,$BC
14897 728A EEEE     	byte >ee,>ee,>ee,>ee,>ec,>ec,>ec,>bc
     728C EEEE     
     728E ECEC     
     7290 ECBC     
14898               	; 	DATA BYTE $EC,$EC,$EC,$BC,$EC,$EC,$EC,$BC
14899 7292 ECEC     	byte >ec,>ec,>ec,>bc,>ec,>ec,>ec,>bc
     7294 ECBC     
     7296 ECEC     
     7298 ECBC     
14900               	; 	DATA BYTE $EC,$EC,$EC,$BC,$EE,$EE,$EE,$EE
14901 729A ECEC     	byte >ec,>ec,>ec,>bc,>ee,>ee,>ee,>ee
     729C ECBC     
     729E EEEE     
     72A0 EEEE

14903               	; esc_deck:	' 6 x (char, 8 colour bytes) -- write into the THIRD-0 colour table at 8192 + char*8, once, at setup

15006               	; 	INCLUDE "store.bas"
15007               	; 	' Keystone Kapers store -- GENERATED by assets/genstore.py. Do not edit.
15008               	; 	'
15009               	; 	' EIGHT band templates of 5 rows x 32 cols (160 bytes each) plus a 32-byte
15010               	; 	' index over (level, screen). The whole 8-screen, 4-level building is about
15011               	; 	' 1.3 KB instead of the 5,120 bytes it would cost to store literally.
15012               	; 	'
15013               	; 	' All eight templates are ONE contiguous block under ONE label, so a band
15014               	; 	' blit is
15015               	; 	'     SCREEN stor_tpl, tpl*160, toprow*32, 32, 5, 32
15016               	; 	' with no label ladder -- SCREEN needs a literal label and a label cannot be
15017               	; 	' chosen by a variable, which is why RallyX needed a 4-way IF over its maze
15018               	; 	' tables. One block sidesteps it.
15019               	; 	'
15020               	; 	' 160 is EVEN, so this cannot leave the location counter odd and misalign
15021               	; 	' the word tables defined after it.
15022               	; 	'
15023               	; 	' template ids:
15024               	; 	'   0 T_AISLE_A
15025               	; 	'   1 T_AISLE_B
15026               	; 	'   2 T_ESC_W
15027               	; 	'   3 T_ESC_E
15028               	; 	'   4 T_ELEV
15029               	; 	'   5 T_ROOF0
15030               	; 	'   6 T_ROOF1
15031               	; 	'   7 T_ROOF2
15032               	; 	'   8 T_ROOF3
15033               	; 	'   9 T_ROOF4
15034               	; 	'   10 T_ROOF5
15035               	; 	'   11 T_ROOF6
15036               	; 	'   12 T_ROOF7
15037               	; 	'   13 T_END_W
15038               	; 	'   14 T_END_E
15039               	;
15040               	; stor_tpl:	' 15 templates x 160 bytes
15041               cvb_STOR_TPL
